Ben and Sian Reynolds. Hassocks: Harvester Press, 1979). A collective volume by French historians containing much that is useful is Constructing the Past: Essays in Historical Methodology (ed. Jacques le Goff and Pierre Nora. Cambridge University Press, 1985). Peter Burke’s History and Social Theory (Oxford: Polity Press, 1993) also contains a discussion of how the notion of ‘structure’ is not understood by historians.

米歇尔·福柯出版的著作，绝非全都可以称为结构主义著作。值得列在这里的是《词与物：人文科学考古学》（The Order of Things: An Archaeology of the Human Sciences；A. M. 谢里丹·史密斯译，伦敦：Tavistock，1974）和《知识考古学》（The Archaeology of Knowledge；A. M. 谢里丹·史密斯译，伦敦：Tavistock，1972）。英语福柯研究著作日益增多，其中最有用的是休伯特·L. 德雷福斯和保罗·拉比诺的《米歇尔·福柯：超越结构主义与解释学》（Michel Foucault: Beyond Structuralism and Hermeneutics；芝加哥大学出版社，1982），以及《福柯：批判读本》（Foucault: A Critical Reader；戴维·库曾斯·霍伊编，纽约：牛津大学出版社，1986）。福柯著作中选材精当的选集有《福柯读本》（A Foucault Reader；保罗·拉比诺编，Harmondsworth：Penguin，1984）。已有两部福柯传记问世：迪迪埃·埃里蓬资料丰富却谨慎的《米歇尔·福柯》（Michel Foucault；贝齐·温译，伦敦：Faber，1991），以及詹姆斯·米勒抱负很大、极具启发性的《米歇尔·福柯的激情》（The Passion of Michel Foucault；伦敦：HarperCollins，1993）。

By no means all of Michel Foucault’s published writings can be described as Structuralist. Those deserving inclusion here are The Order of Things: An Archaeology of the Human Sciences (trans. A. M. Sheridan Smith. London: Tavistock, 1974), and The Archaeology of Knowledge (trans. A. M. Sheridan Smith. London: Tavistock, 1972). Of the growing number of books written on Foucault in English, the most useful are Hubert L. Dreyfus and Paul Rabinow, Michel Foucault: Beyond Structuralism and Hermeneutics (Chicago University Press, 1982), and Foucault: A Critical Reader (ed. David Couzens Hoy. New York: Oxford University Press, 1986). For a well-chosen anthology of Foucault’s writings: A Foucault Reader (ed. Paul Rabinow. Harmondsworth: Penguin, 1984). Two biographies of Foucault have appeared: Didier Eribon’s informative but cautious Michel Foucault (trans. Betsy Wing. London: Faber, 1991), and James Miller’s ambitious and highly enlightening The Passion of Michel Foucault (London: HarperCollins, 1993).

政治。阿尔都塞并不是多产的作家。他的主要论文收入《保卫马克思》（For Marx；本·布鲁斯特译，伦敦：Verso，1969）和《读〈资本论〉》（Reading Capital；本·布鲁斯特译，伦敦：Verso，1970）。他的非凡自传讲述了其情感生活中极为坎坷的故事，以及哲学观念的生成，英译本题为《未来持续很久》（The Future Lasts a Long Time；理查德·维西译，伦敦：Chatto and Windus，1993）。

Politics. Althusser was not a productive writer. His major essays are collected in For Marx (trans. Ben Brewster. London: Verso, 1969), and Reading Capital (trans. Ben Brewster. London: Verso, 1970). His extraordinary autobiography, recounting the very troubled story of his emotional life and the genesis of his philosophical ideas, appeared in English under the title The Future Lasts a Long Time (trans. Richard Veasey. London: Chatto and Windus, 1993).

精神分析。西格蒙德·弗洛伊德的人类心理理论，确实可以使他被称为早期结构主义者。通过《精神分析引论》（Introductory Lectures on Psychoanalysis；琼·里维埃译，伦敦：Allen and Unwin，1922），或《梦的解析》（The Interpretation of Dreams；詹姆斯·斯特雷奇译，伦敦：Allen and Unwin，1954）的方法论部分，可以很好地了解其主要思想。弗洛伊德著名的法国弟子雅克·拉康的著作，以艰深难懂著称。他的重要论文选集见《文集选》（Ecrits: A Selection；艾伦·谢里丹译，伦敦：Tavistock，1977）。更易读的是《精神分析的四个基本概念》（The Four Fundamental Concepts of Psycho-analysis；艾伦·谢里丹译，伦敦：Hogarth Press，1977），以及《研讨班第一、二卷》（The Seminar, Books One and Two；约翰·福雷斯特和西尔瓦娜·托马塞利译，剑桥大学出版社，1988）。英语拉康研究著作不断增多。较有启发性的包括 E. 拉格兰德—沙利文《雅克·拉康与精神分析哲学》（Jacques Lacan and the Philosophy of Psychoanalysis；伦敦：Croom Helm，1986）、肖莎娜·费尔曼《雅克·拉康与洞见的冒险》（Jacques Lacan and the Adventure of Insight；哈佛大学出版社，1987），以及戴维·梅西《语境中的拉康》（Lacan in Contexts；伦敦：Verso，1988）。但最好的拉康思想简明阐述，远远当属马尔科姆·鲍伊在“现代大师”丛书中的《拉康》（Lacan；伦敦：Fontana Press，1991）。

Psychoanalysis. Sigmund Freud may certainly be claimed as a proto-Structuralist in his theories of the human psyche. Good access to his main ideas may be got from his Introductory Lectures on Psychoanalysis (trans. Joan Riviere. London: Allen and Unwin, 1922), or from the methodological sections of The Interpretation of Dreams (trans. James Strachey. London: Allen and Unwin, 1954). The writings of Freud’s celebrated French disciple, Jacques Lacan, are notoriously difficult. A selection of his major essays is contained in Ecrits: A Selection (trans. Alan Sheridan. London: Tavistock, 1977). More accessible are The Four Fundamental Concepts of Psycho-analysis (trans. Alan Sheridan. London: Hogarth Press, 1977), and The Seminar, Books One and Two (trans. John Forrester and Silvana Tomaselli. Cambridge University Press, 1988). Books about Lacan in English have proliferated. Among the more instructive ones are E. Ragland-Sullivan, Jacques Lacan and the Philosophy of Psychoanalysis (London: Croom Helm, 1986), Soshana Felman: Jacques Lacan and the Adventure of Insight (Harvard University Press, 1987), and David Macey, Lacan in Contexts (London: Verso, 1988). But far and away the best short exposition of Lacan’s thought is in Malcolm Bowie’s Modern Master, Lacan (London: Fontana Press, 1991).

符号学Semiotics

符号学的经典文本见于美国哲学家 C. S. 皮尔士的著作。主要文本可以在其《论文集》（Collected Papers）第2卷找到（查尔斯·哈茨霍恩和保罗·韦斯编，哈佛大学出版社，1978）。皮尔士最著名的美国追随者是查尔斯·莫里斯，其符号学论文汇集于《符号的一般理论文集》（Writings on the General Theory of Signs；海牙：Mouton，1971）。欧洲这一领域的主要作者是意大利人翁贝托·埃科。他的《符号学理论》（A Theory of Semiotics；印第安纳大学出版社，1975）是一部专业而详细的综合论著；《符号学与语言哲学》（Semiotics and the Philosophy of Language；印第安纳大学出版社，1984）也兼具生动性和全面性。罗兰·巴特的《符号学原理》（Elements of Semiology；安妮特·拉弗斯和 C. 史密斯译，伦敦：Cape，1967）及《时尚系统》（The Fashion System；伦敦：Cape，1984）更具个人特色，但他的《神话集》（Mythologies；安妮特·拉弗斯译，伦敦：Cape，1972）是符号学如何应用于当代文化阐释的绝佳范例。《符号学：入门选集》（Semiotics: An Introductory Anthology；罗伯特·E. 英尼斯编，Hutchinson，1986）出色地汇集了从皮尔士至今主要理论家的文本。A. J. 格雷马斯和 J. 库尔泰的《符号学与语言》（Semiotics and Language；印第安纳大学出版社，1982）程度更深，但也是有用的参考著作。

The classical texts in Semiotics are contained in the writings of the American philosopher C. S. Peirce. The main ones are to be found in Volume 2 of his Collected Papers (ed. Charles Hartshorne and Paul Weiss. Harvard University Press, 1978). Peirce’s most prominent American follower was Charles Morris, whose essays on Semiotics are brought together in Writings on the General Theory of Signs (The Hague: Mouton, 1971). The leading European writer on the subject has been the Italian, Umberto Eco. His A Theory of Semiotics (Indiana University Press, 1975) is an expert and detailed general account; Semiotics and the Philosophy of Language (Indiana University Press, 1984) is also both lively and comprehensive. Roland Barthes’s Elements of Semiology (trans. Annette Lavers and C. Smith. London: Cape, 1967), and The Fashion System (London: Cape, 1984) are more idiosyncratic, but his Mythologies (trans. Annette Lavers. London: Cape, 1972) is a superb example of how Semiotics can be applied to the interpretation of contemporary culture. An excellent collection of texts by the major theoreticians from Peirce to the present is Semiotics: An Introductory Anthology (ed. Robert E. Innis. Hutchinson, 1986). More advanced but a useful reference-work is A. J. Greimas and J. Courtès, Semiotics and Language (Indiana University Press, 1982).

文学Literature

俄国形式主义者著作中，享有声誉的选集仍然只有《俄国诗学选读》（Readings in Russian Poetics；L. 马泰伊卡和 K. 波莫尔斯卡编，马萨诸塞州剑桥：麻省理工学院出版社，1971）。形式主义专著方面，维克托·什克洛夫斯基的《散文理论》（Theory of Prose；1925）终于在1990年有了英译本（本杰明·谢尔译，伊利诺伊州 Elmwood Park：Dalkey Archive Press）；弗拉基米尔·普罗普的《民间故事形态学》（Morphology of the Folktale；得克萨斯大学出版社，1968）则长期是叙事研究者的重要来源。维克托·埃利希的《俄国形式主义：历史与学说》（Russian Formalism: History, Doctrine；耶鲁大学出版社，1981）全面而翔实地介绍了形式主义运动。

The one reputable anthology of the writings of the Russian Formalists remains Readings in Russian Poetics (ed. L. Matejka and K. Pomorska. Cambridge, Mass: MIT Press, 1971). Of Formalist monographs, Viktor Shklovsky’s Theory of Prose (1925) finally became available in English in 1990 (trans. Benjamin Sher. Elmwood Park, IL: Dalkey Archive Press); while Vladimir Propp’s Morphology of the Folktale (University of Texas Press, 1968) has long been a prime source for students of narrative. There is a full and informative account of the Formalist movement in Victor Erlich, Russian Formalism: History, Doctrine (Yale University Press, 1981).

要理解从形式主义向成熟结构主义的过渡，一个好的起点，是米哈伊尔·巴赫金和 P. M. 梅德韦杰夫的《文学研究中的形式方法》（The Formal Method in Literary Scholarship；阿尔伯特·J. 韦尔勒译，巴尔的摩：约翰斯·霍普金斯大学出版社，1978）对形式主义方法的犀利批评。巴赫金的其他著作中，从结构主义角度看，也许最具启发性的是《对话的想象》（The Dialogic Imagination；C. 埃默森和迈克尔·霍尔奎斯特译，得克萨斯大学出版社，1981）中的长文《小说中的话语》。茨维坦·托多罗夫《米哈伊尔·巴赫金：对话原则》（Mikhail Bakhtin: The Dialogical Principle；弗拉德·戈齐奇译，曼彻斯特大学出版社，1984）清楚而全面地介绍了巴赫金的文学思想。卡捷琳娜·克拉克和迈克尔·霍尔奎斯特合著的思想传记《米哈伊尔·巴赫金》（Mikhail Bakhtin；哈佛大学出版社，1984），也有许多令人感兴趣的内容。

To understand the transition from Formalism to a full-blown Structuralism, a good starting-point is the incisive critique of Formalist method contained in Mikhail Bakhtin and P. M. Medvedev, The Formal Method in Literary Scholarship (trans. Albert J. Wehrle. Baltimore: Johns Hopkins University Press, 1978). Of Bakhtin’s other writings, perhaps the most enlightening from a Structuralist viewpoint is the long essay on ‘Discourse in the Novel’ in The Dialogic Imagination (trans. C. Emerson and Michael Holquist. University of Texas Press, 1981). A clear and comprehensive account of Bakhtin’s literary thought can be found in Tzvetan Todorov, Mikhail Bakhtin: The Dialogical Principle (trans. Wlad Godzich. Manchester University Press, 1984). There is much of interest also in the intellectual biography by Katerina Clark and Michael Holquist: Mikhail Bakhtin (Harvard University Press, 1984).

布拉格学派在20世纪30年代对文学结构主义及符号学的发展有很大影响。F. W. 加兰的《历史结构：布拉格学派计划，1928–1946》（Historic Structures: The Prague School Project 1928–1946；伦敦：Croom Helm，1985）对此作了出色的介绍。该学派发表的著作已选编为《布拉格学派：著作选，1929–1946》（The Prague School: Selected Writings, 1929–1946；彼得·施泰纳编，得克萨斯大学出版社，1982）。罗曼·雅各布森的大部分文学论文收入其《著作选》（Selected Writings）第二、三卷（海牙：Mouton）。最佳单卷合集是《罗曼·雅各布森：文学中的语言》（Roman Jakobson: Language in Literature；克里斯蒂娜·波莫尔斯卡和斯蒂芬·鲁迪编，哈佛大学出版社，1988）。他关于“语言学与诗学”的主要论述，见《语言中的风格》（Style in Language；托马斯·A. 西比奥克编，马萨诸塞州剑桥：麻省理工学院出版社，1960）。其文学与语言学论文还有一部较短的选集《言语艺术、言语符号、言语时间》（Verbal Art, Verbal Sign, Verbal Time；克里斯蒂娜·波莫尔斯卡和斯蒂芬·鲁迪编，牛津：Blackwell，1985）；《罗曼·雅各布森与克里斯蒂娜·波莫尔斯卡对话录》（Dialogues between Roman Jakobson and Krystyna Pomorska；克里斯蒂安·休伯特译，剑桥大学出版社，1983）则把个人回忆与理论探讨巧妙地结合起来。

The work of the Prague School, which was very influential in the development of literary Structuralism and of semiotics in the 1930s, is admirably described in F. W. Galan’s Historic Structures: The Prague School Project 1928–1946 (London: Croom Helm, 1985). An anthology of the School’s published work has appeared as The Prague School: Selected Writings, 1929–1946 (ed. Peter Steiner. University of Texas Press, 1982). Most of Roman Jakobson’s essays on literature are contained in Volumes Two and Three of his Selected Writings (The Hague: Mouton). The best collection of them in a single volume is Roman Jakobson: Language in Literature (ed. Krystyna Pomorska and Stephen Rudy. Harvard University Press, 1988). His major pronouncement on ‘Linguistics and Poetics’ appears in Style in Language (ed. Thomas A. Sebeok. Cambridge, Mass: MIT Press, 1960). A shorter selection of his literary and linguistic essays has been published as Verbal Art, Verbal Sign, Verbal Time (ed. Krystyna Pomorska and Stephen Rudy. Oxford: Blackwell, 1985), and there is an attractive mixture of personal reminiscence and theorising in Dialogues between Roman Jakobson and Krystyna Pomorska (trans. Christian Hubert. Cambridge University Press, 1983).

文学结构主义的综合论著中，有两部突出之作：罗伯特·斯科尔斯《文学中的结构主义：导论》（Structuralism in Literature: An Introduction；耶鲁大学出版社，1974）；乔纳森·卡勒《结构主义诗学：结构主义、语言学与文学研究》（Structuralist Poetics: Structuralism, Linguistics and the Study of Literature；伦敦：Routledge，1975）。戴维·洛奇的《运用结构主义：十九、二十世纪文学论文与评论》（Working with Structuralism: Essays and Reviews on Nineteenth and Twentieth-Century Literature；伦敦：Routledge，1981）是一部非常易读的文集，作者是一位英国小说家兼批评家，对批评中的结构主义方法特别乐于接受。

Among general surveys of literary Structuralism, two stand out: Robert Scholes’s Structuralism in Literature: An Introduction (Yale University Press, 1974); and Jonathan Culler’s Structuralist Poetics: Structuralism, Linguistics and the Study of Literature (London: Routledge, 1975). David Lodge’s Working with Structuralism: Essays and Reviews on Nineteenth and Twentieth-Century Literature (London: Routledge, 1981) is a very readable collection of pieces by a British novelist and critic peculiarly receptive to Structuralist methods in criticism.

法国方面，罗兰·巴特的某些著作非常重要：《批评论文集》（Critical Essays；理查德·霍华德译，伊利诺伊州埃文斯顿：西北大学出版社，1972）；《批评与真理》（Criticism and Truth；卡特琳·科伊内曼译，伦敦：Athlone Press，1987）；《S/Z》（理查德·米勒译，伦敦：Cape，1975）；以及《图像、音乐、文本》（Image, Music, Text；斯蒂芬·希思编译，伦敦：Fontana Press，1977）中的部分论文。乔纳森·卡勒在“现代大师”丛书中的《巴特》（Barthes；伦敦：Fontana Press，1983）也很出色。最有力地推进结构主义诗学研究的两位法国理论家，是茨维坦·托多罗夫和热拉尔·热奈特。托多罗夫的英语著作有《诗学导论》（Introduction to Poetics；理查德·霍华德译，Hassocks：Harvester Press，1981）和《散文诗学》（The Poetics of Prose；理查德·霍华德译，康奈尔大学出版社，1990）。热奈特的英译本包括《叙事话语：方法论》（Narrative Discourse: An Essay in Method；简·E. 卢因译，康奈尔大学出版社，1980）和《再论叙事话语》（Narrative Discourse Revisited；简·E. 卢因译，康奈尔大学出版社，1988）。第三位对结构主义文学理论贡献很大的法国理论家，是 A. J. 格雷马斯。他的《结构语义学》（Structural Semantics；D. 麦克道尔等译，内布拉斯加大学出版社，1983）有些地方难读，但最终颇有收获。

On the French side, certain of the writings of Roland Barthes are of major importance: Critical Essays (trans. Richard Howard. Evanston, Il: Northwestern University Press, 1972); Criticism and Truth (trans. Katrine Keuneman. London: Athlone Press, 1987); S/Z (trans. Richard Miller. London: Cape, 1975); and some of the essays in Image, Music, Text (ed. and trans. Stephen Heath. London: Fontana Press, 1977). There is an excelent Modern Master volume by Jonathan Culler, Barthes (London: Fontana Press, 1983). The two French theorists who have advanced the study of Structuralist Poetics the most are Tzvetan Todorov and Gérard Genette. For Todorov in English, there is Introduction to Poetics (trans. Richard Howard. Hassocks: Harvester Press, 1981) and The Poetics of Prose (trans. Richard Howard. Cornell University Press, 1990). Translations of Genette include Narrative Discourse: An Essay in Method (trans. Jane E. Lewin. Cornell University Press, 1980) and Narrative Discourse Revisited (trans. Jane E. Lewin. Cornell University Press, 1988). A third French theorist who has contributed much to Structuralist literary theory is A. J. Greimas. His Structural Semantics (trans. D. McDowell and others. University of Nebraska Press, 1983) is difficult in places but ultimately rewarding.

后结构主义Post-Structuralism

这里的必读书是雅克·德里达的早期著作，尤其是《声音与现象及论胡塞尔符号理论的其他论文》（Speech and Phenomena, and Other Essays on Husserl’s Theory of Signs；戴维·B. 艾利森译，伊利诺伊州埃文斯顿：西北大学出版社，1973）；《论文字学》（Of Grammatology；加亚特里·斯皮瓦克译，巴尔的摩：约翰斯·霍普金斯大学出版社，1976），其中包含他对索绪尔的长篇批判，在此语境下至关重要；《书写与差异》（Writing and Difference；艾伦·巴斯译，伦敦：Routledge，1978）；《哲学的边缘》（Margins of Philosophy；艾伦·巴斯译，芝加哥大学出版社，1982）。德里达20世纪70年代后期和80年代的写作，不那么直接具有哲学性或理论性，在这里不必考虑。对其思想最好的一种简短、尚算浅易的介绍，是克里斯托弗·诺里斯《德里达》（Derrida；伦敦：Fontana Press，1987）。另两部值得读、客观的哲学研究是：约翰·卢埃林《意义门槛上的德里达》（Derrida on the Threshold of Sense；伦敦：Macmillan，1986）；以及虽远非易读的鲁道夫·加舍《镜背的镀层》（The Tain of the Mirror；哈佛大学出版社，1986）。

The essential reading here is the earlier writings of Jacques Derrida, notably Speech and Phenomena, and Other Essays on Husserl’s Theory of Signs (trans. David B. Allison. Evanston, Il: Northwestern University Press, 1973); Of Grammatology (trans. Gayatri Spivak. Baltimore: Johns Hopkins University Press, 1976), which contains his long and in this context crucial critique of Saussure; Writing and Difference (trans. Alan Bass. London: Routledge, 1978); and Margins of Philosophy (trans. Alan Bass. University of Chicago Press, 1982). Derrida’s writings of the later 1970s and 1980s are less directly philosophical or theoretical, and need not be taken into account here. The best short, reasonably simple account of his thought is Christopher Norris: Derrida (London: Fontana Press, 1987). Two other worthwhile and objective philosophical studies are: John Llewellyn: Derrida on the Threshold of Sense (London: Macmillan, 1986); and, though it makes far from easy reading, Rodolphe Gasché: The Tain of the Mirror (Harvard University Press, 1986).

在美国，后结构主义以“解构”之名更为人知，这是一种激进的文学思想流派，很快产生了大量支持和反对它的学术文献。美国杰出的解构主义者是保罗·德曼，他精微而有时难以捉摸的作品见于《盲目与洞见：当代批评修辞论文集》（Blindness and Insight: Essays in the Rhetoric of Contemporary Criticism；修订版，伦敦：Methuen，1983）、《阅读的寓言：卢梭、尼采、里尔克和普鲁斯特中的比喻语言》（Allegories of Reading: Figural Language in Rousseau, Nietzsche, Rilke, and Proust；耶鲁大学出版社，1979）、《对理论的抵抗》（The Resistance to Theory；明尼苏达大学出版社，1986），以及《批评文集，1953–1978》（Critical Writings 1953–1978；林赛·沃特斯编，明尼苏达大学出版社，1989）。另一位受解构影响的重要美国批评家是杰弗里·哈特曼，尤其可参看《荒野中的批评：今日文学研究》（Criticism in the Wilderness; The Study of Literature Today；耶鲁大学出版社，1980）。清楚介绍解构的综合论著包括：乔纳森·卡勒《论解构：结构主义之后的理论与批评》（On Deconstruction: Theory and Criticism after Structuralism；伦敦：Routledge，1983）；克里斯托弗·诺里斯《解构：理论与实践》（Deconstruction: Theory and Practice；伦敦：Methuen，1982）；文森特·B. 利奇《解构批评：进阶导论》（Deconstructive Criticism: An Advanced Introduction；伦敦：Hutchinson，1983）。反面回应可参看约翰·M. 埃利斯《反对解构》（Against Deconstruction；普林斯顿大学出版社，1989）。

In the United States Post-Structuralism is better known under the name of Deconstruction, a radical school of literary thought that has quickly given rise to a huge academic literature, both for and against. The outstanding US deconstructionist was Paul de Man, whose subtle and sometimes elusive work is contained in Blindness and Insight: Essays in the Rhetoric of Contemporary Criticism (revised ed. London: Methuen, 1983); Allegories of Reading: Figural Language in Rousseau, Nietzsche, Rilke, and Proust (Yale University Press, 1979); The Resistance to Theory (University of Minnesota Press, 1986); and Critical Writings 1953–1978 (ed. Lindsay Waters. University of Minnesota Press, 1989). Another major American critic influenced by Deconstruction is Geoffrey Hartman: see especially his Criticism in the Wilderness; The Study of Literature Today (Yale University Press, 1980). Lucid general accounts of Deconstruction are: Jonathan Culler’s On Deconstruction: Theory and Criticism after Structuralism (London: Routledge, 1983), Christopher Norris’s Deconstruction: Theory and Practice (London: Methuen, 1982); and Vincent B. Leitch’s Deconstructive Criticism: An Advanced Introduction (London: Hutchinson, 1983). For a negative response: John M. Ellis: Against Deconstruction (Princeton University Press, 1989).

第1章　语言Chapter 1 Language

3. 《教程》的第一个英译本由韦德·巴斯金翻译，1959年在英国和美国出版。第二个改进的译本由牛津大学的（有史以来第一位！）普通语言学教授罗伊·哈里斯翻译，1983年出版。本书引文采用第二个译本，但在术语上有一项重要的偏离——参见本章注17。

This second translation is the one I have used in the present book from which to quote, with one important terminological departure – see Note 17 to the present chapter.

9. 罗伊·哈里斯在其《教程》译本的导言中，谈到将 langue 译成一个适用于它在法语中所有用法的英语词有多困难，提出了一些有趣的意见（第xv–xvi页）。用“language”和“speech”对应索绪尔的 langue 和 parole，仍有很多不足。重要的是记住，这一对术语之间的关系，是“系统”或“抽象结构”与“具体事件”之间的关系。

9. In his introduction to his translation of the Cours Roy Harris makes some interesting remarks on the difficulty of translating the term langue into a single English term suitable for all its uses in French (pp. xv–xvi). ‘Language’ and ‘speech’, as the equivalents of Saussure’s langue and parole, leave a lot to be desired. The important thing to remember is that this pair of terms stand to one another in the relation of ‘system’ or ‘abstract structure’ to ‘concrete event’.

15. L. Wittgenstein, Philosophical Investigations, p. 50.

↩

16. 符号与词不应等同，因为语言系统中有些符号并不是词（例如标点）。如果符号是“意义单位”，显然词的组成部分也可以算作符号，因为词的部分可以脱离其余部分而有意义。例如，前缀和后缀承载意义，并作为独立单位出现在词典中。精确定义什么是“词”极为困难。我不作这种尝试。

16. Signs and words should not be equated, since there are signs in a language-system which are not words (marks of punctuation, for instance). If a sign is a ‘unit of meaning’, it is clear that parts of words can also be counted as signs, since parts of words can mean in isolation from the rest of the word. Prefixes and suffixes, for example, bear meaning and occur in dictionaries as independent units. To define what exactly a ‘word’ is is extremely difficult. I do not attempt it.

↩

17. 罗伊·哈里斯在《教程》译本中，把 significant 和 signifié 分别译作“signal”（信号）和“signification”（意义）。这打破了英语中将其译为“signifier”（能指）和“signified”（所指）的传统（尽管这一传统确实不长）。哈里斯没有为这个新做法说明理由，我在本书引用他的译文时，选择恢复原来的译法。在我看来，“signifier”和“signified”比哈里斯所建议的术语更有优势：它们陌生，并且是专为分析语言或其他符号而创造的。两者之间的对照强于“signal”与“signification”；后两词在其他语境中很熟悉，因此我认为容易造成混淆。

17. In his translation of the Cours, Roy Harris translates significant and signifié as ‘signal’ and ‘signification’ respectively. This is a break with the (admittedly short) tradition in English of translating them as ‘signifier’ and ‘signified’. Harris does not justify this novelty and I have chosen in the present book to reverse it where quoting from his translation. The terms ‘signifier’ and ‘signified’ seem to me to have the advantage over those suggested by Harris of strangeness and of having been created for the actual purpose of analysing a linguistic or other sign. The contrast between them is stronger than that between ‘signal’ and ‘signification’, which are terms familiar from other contexts and therefore liable, I believe, to produce confusion.

↩

18. R. Barthes, Essais critiques, p. 214.

12. 英译标题“The Savage Mind”（字面意为“野蛮的心智”）是原题《野性的思维》（La Pensée sauvage）的一种误导性翻译，因为它完全没有传达法语 sauvage 中“非官方”的含义（例如，“未经工会批准的罢工”在法语中是 une grève sauvage）。有人提出，“untamed”（未驯化的）才是列维-斯特劳斯所用 sauvage 的最佳英译。“The Savage Mind”尤其不幸，因为它强化了列维-斯特劳斯一心要从西方人头脑中消除的倾向：把“原始”民族看作低劣、未开化。

12. ‘The Savage Mind’ is a misleading translation of the original title, La Pensée sauvage, because it fails altogether to convey the meaning of ‘unofficial’ present in the French term sauvage (the French for a ‘wildcat strike’, e.g., is ‘une grève sauvage’). ‘Untamed’ is a suggestion which has been put forward as the best English translation of ‘sauvage’ as Lévi-Strauss is using it. ‘The Savage Mind’ is especially unfortunate for reinforcing the very tendency Lévi-Strauss is so set on removing from Western minds: that which sees ‘primitive’ peoples as inferior and uncivilized.

↩

13. 列维-斯特劳斯，《忧郁的热带》（Tristes tropiques），第153页。

13. Lévi-Strauss, Tristes tropiques, p. 153.

第3章　符号学Chapter 3 Semiotics

1. 参见丹·斯珀伯在《结构主义及其后》（Structuralism and Since；J. 斯特罗克编）中对此的简短评论，第23–4页。

1. See the brief comments on this by Dan Sperber in Structuralism and Since, ed. J. Sturrock, pp. 23–4.

↩

2. 有些症状也可能就是疾病本身；皮肤上的斑痕被确认为所谓湿疹时，这些斑痕本身就构成该疾病。在这种情况下，把这些符号看作“症状”，是不是错了？

2. Some symptoms may also be the illness; the marks on the skin which turn out to be the condition known as eczema themselves constitute the condition. Are we wrong in such instances to take the signs to be ‘symptoms’?

↩

12. 索绪尔和皮尔士对“象征”（symbol）一词的用法不同，可能引起混淆。皮尔士的定义很精确，通常为人们采用；索绪尔的用法则宽泛，因为他没有任何符号类型学。他使用“象征”一词的方式，与今天英语中仍广泛使用的方式相同，指我们认作具有“象征性”的那些符号：十字架作为基督教的象征，米字旗作为英国的象征，等等。

12. Confusion may occur because of the different usages of the term ‘symbol’ by Saussure and by Peirce. Peirce’s definition is precise and is the one generally adopted; Saussure’s usage is loose because he did not have any typology of signs. He uses the term ‘symbol’ as it is still widely used in English today, to refer to those signs which we recognize to be ‘symbolic’: the cross as the symbol of Christianity, the Union Jack as the symbol of Britain, and so on.

17. 使问题更复杂的是，丘吉尔式V字手势也是一种戏仿，因为它是英国仍广泛使用的粗鲁V字手势的礼貌改造。丘吉尔通过反转手指的位置，使指关节朝内而非朝外，完成这一改造；人们还会把它额外“读作”旧式V字手势，用来针对希特勒和纳粹德国。

17. The Churchillian V-sign is also a parody, to complicate matters further, being a polite adaptation of the ruder kind of V-sign still widely used in Britain. The Churchillian adaptation, effected by reversing the position of the fingers so that the knuckles face inwards instead of outwards, will have been ‘read’ additionally as being a V-sign of the older kind directed against Hitler and against Nazi Germany.

结论Conclusion

1. 例如，福柯在声望卓著的法兰西学院的就职演讲中，以如下自我否认开篇：“与其发言[prendre la parole]，我更愿意被言语包围……与其成为话语由之而来的那个人，我宁愿任其展开所摆布。”

1. In his inaugural lecture at the prestigious Collège de France, Foucault for instance began with the disclaimer: ‘Rather than giving voice [prendre la parole], I would have like to have been enveloped by it . . . instead of being the one from whom discourse comes, I would rather have been at the mercy of its unfolding.’
